Component · labeling-server (+ labeling-web for web/)

The hub: labeling tracker, registry and training front door

After this page you can name every hub page and API family, know which table records what, how the hub is run, tested and deployed, and where its cron jobs and logs are.

Last verified 2026-10-01 · CONTRACT v1.7 · owner: labeling-server, labeling-web

What it is for

The hub is the system of record for the labeling platform and its lineage: which series are to be labeled, by whom, what was delivered and reviewed, which labels went into which training split, which run produced which weights, how those weights evaluated and in which release they shipped. It is also the only user interface: labelers' work arrives from OncoStudio, leads assign and review, the data team trains models through it (the executor has no UI of its own) and reads ai-pipeline's regulatory figures through it. It never runs training itself, never writes DICOM to the central PACS, never stores patient names, and serves this documentation at /docs/.

Pages (navigation)

Scopes are explained on Roles: own = a labeler, data = leads and read-only viewers, admin, public = not logged in. Navigation since v1.6 (D95/D96): leads, viewers and admins see 학습 → 라벨링 → 기록 → 관리 → 도움말 and land on 학습 › 작업 after login; a labeler sees 내 작업 · 계정 · 문서 only and lands on 내 작업. Pages that left the menu keep their address.

Page (menu)ScopeWhat it showsGuide
공개 현황 (public overview, / logged out) — retired by v1.55 §72: the public view is the shell with 개요 + the public 배포 tabs; GET /ui/api/public/overview stays one version, unusedpublicproject progress, releases, pipeline pin, docs linkRoles
기록 (records) /records?tab=live|patients|deliverables|labelers|sessions|analytics|tasksdataone page hosting the former monitors as tabs, unchanged (D96): 실시간 (who is labeling what right now, the event feed, alerts, platform strip — the same live SSE), 환자 현황 (claims and overlaps), 결과물 (every saved RTSTRUCT with its timeline), 작업자 (per-labeler statistics), 세션, 분석 (throughput and quality charts), 할당 (the legacy task list). The old routes (/live, /patients, …, the kiosk /live?kiosk=1) still answer for deep links and testsLabeling, records
프로젝트 (projects), project pagedata (own: name + ROI list only, plus roi_count and mine)projects, assignment board, policy and pre-label panel. For a labeler (own scope) GET /ui/api/projects lists only the non-archived projects with an assignment for them, each row {id, code, name, roi_set, roi_count, mine:{open, labeled, review, approved}}, where mine counts their own assignments per v1.18 display stage (open = assigned + in progress; nobody else's numbers). GET /ui/api/projects/:id gives them the same row as the page header (403 on a project with nothing of theirs); the board and the other project panels stay data-only (UX review W1). The web shows a labeler these rows as cards of their own (D153 W1): code, name, region · modality and the finished tag when sent, the notes, the ROI count, their work per stage (Assigned / Labeled / Review / Approved pills) and a 내 작업 n건 link to /my?project=<id> (내 작업 narrowed to that project, a chip clears it); the status / region filters are hidden (the search filters the cards); nothing the scope does not receive is drawn. Facet values read in the UI language everywhere (sex 남 / 여, contrast 조영 / 비조영, split 학습 / 검증 / 미지정, unknown 미상; locales facet.*), and a unit test fails on any page key missing in ko or en (web/tests/locales.test.mjs), the layout check on any rendered key (web/e2e/layout.mjs). 새 프로젝트 and 시리즈 추가 are steppers with a confirm step (D153 W8, Stepper.svelte, checks in lib/projectForm.js, e2e web/e2e/wizard.mjs). The add-series stepper (lead) previews before it commits: POST /ui/api/projects/:id/assignments/preview takes the body of …/assignments ({items:[{image_series_uid, patient_id?}]}, ≤ 2000) and writes nothing (a read-only transaction); per item, in order: {image_series_uid, valid, known, on_board:null|{status, stage}, patient_id, modality, series_description, study_date, facets:{sex, age, contrast, vendor}} — valid = a DICOM UID, known = the hub has the series, on_board = already a row of this project; facets bucketed as the board shows them; no patient names (D153 W8). A project created or edited with an empty name gets its code as the name (web and /api/v1). Delete / undelete (v1.19 §13, D157): POST /api/v1/projects/{code}/delete / /undelete (body {user_id, admin_password}) and the web twins POST /ui/api/projects/:id/delete / /undelete ({admin_password}): delete needs a lead or admin, undelete an admin, both the admin action password — checked against ADMIN_ACTION_PASSWORD_HASH (argon2) in the hub's secrets env, never stored or logged elsewhere; wrong → 403 admin password, the sixth wrong try within a minute → 429, no hash configured → 503, 409 when already (not) deleted. Soft delete (migration 033: status deleted, deleted_at, deleted_by, status_before): the project leaves every list, count, feed (approved-labels, approved-set, label-set → 404), queue, my-work, poll, policy, round tick and sync selection; its assignments are hidden with it (verdicts / comments on them 404); cases, revisions, reviews and label files stay. Undelete restores the previous status; admins list deleted projects with GET /ui/api/projects?status=deleted. Web (labeling-web, v1.19): the project page's 삭제… (lead, shown only when GET /api/v1/health says ≥ 1.19, hidden after a 404) opens DeleteProjectModal.svelte — what happens, the code typed back, the masked password (never stored, cleared after every answer; 403 → 관리자 비밀번호가 틀립니다, 429 → wait retry_after, 503 → not configured) — and returns to the list; admins get a 삭제됨 filter whose cards (not links: a deleted project's page is 404) carry 복원 (rules in lib/projectDelete.js, web/tests/projectDelete.test.mjs). Label checks (v1.19 §26, D159): the board, my-work and review rows' checks:{n_error, n_warning, computed_at}|null show as icon + counts (ChecksCell.svelte; null = 미검사, absent on an older hub = no column) on the board (sortable ?sort=checks, filter ?checks=1, on the loaded rows) and the review queue; the drawer's 라벨 검사 section reads GET /ui/api/assignments/:id/checks; the project's 검사 tab reads GET /ui/api/projects/:id/checks and saves POST …/checks/settings {min_samples} (5–500, lead) (ChecksPanel.svelte, lib/checks.js, web/tests/checks.test.mjs, e2e web/e2e/checks.mjs; locales checks.*, pdelete.*, pstatus.deleted). Workflow (v1.20 §27, hub ≥ 1.20): selection rule line + editor (SelectionRuleDialog.svelte, RuleLine.svelte, lib/selectionRule.js), 항목 추가 by rule (AddByRuleDialog.svelte → …/cases/by-rule), Shift / Ctrl selection, 담당자 지정 / 무작위 배정 (AssignDialog.svelte → …/assign, people from GET /ui/api/users via lib/people.svelte.js), bulk 리뷰어, 자동 윤곽 (AutocontourDialog.svelte), 중앙 PACS 전송 (SendCentralDialog.svelte), the drawer's reviewer / open choice / send / reopen; rules in lib/workflow.js and stage Sent in lib/stages.js (web/tests/workflow.test.mjs, selectionRule.test.mjs, e2e web/e2e/workflow.mjs; locales wf.*, rule.*, byrule.*, scope.*, stage.sent)Labeling
프로젝트 › 라운드 (Rounds tab), round page /projects/:id/rounds/:ndata (actions: lead; valid-set reset: admin)rounds, pool facet bars, sampler preview and commit, validation set, pre-label, progress, training (round jobs ask for no pre-label push, D89; a failed job's DSC is shown), metrics trendRounds

내 작업 (my work) /myown, datav1.20 §27.3 (hub ≥ 1.20): two lists from to_label / to_review of GET /ui/api/my/work — 라벨할 항목 (stage, checks + label file, assigned by, due) and 검수할 항목 (rows whose set reviewer is me: labeled by / when, reviewer since); an older hub: the one list below. The signed-in labeler's queue in OncoStudio's 다음 환자 order: stage (v1.18 display stage, D149; review-note mark) · patient · project (round chip, AI pre-label mark) · series (modality · description · study date, UID tail on hover / copy — D153 W3, from the modality / study_date row fields) · due; cards below 900 px, the drawer docked beside the list at ≥ 1280 px and opening with the row's next step (D153 W4). Every web list, count and history line shows stages, never the stored status (locales stage.* / stage_note.*, Stages). For the own scope no ROI counts and no reviewer names (the review text is in the drawer); the drawer keeps the ROI set vs delivered diff, hub reviews, comments and the add-comment box, with Excel-era reviews and notes under a collapsed 이전 기록 (Excel) block (H4). Unbound → a hint to 계정. Rows of GET /ui/api/my/work and the drawer's assignment carry modality and study_date (from the image series, null when unknown) for the series line modality · description · date (D153 W3)Labeling

v1.6 additions — labeling workspace API (§12, D92–D96; app/api_workspace.py)OncoStudio's Labeling tab. The scope is the D76 matrix of the hub account the user_id login is bound to (user_alias → web_user); an unbound login gets user_bound:false and empty lists. GET /api/v1/me (own · data · admin, unbound) · GET /api/v1/review/queue (data · admin; own → 403 not available to your role) · POST /api/v1/assignments/{id}/start-review, …/review (role ≥ lead; the same review row as the web page, origin='hub', reviewer = the bound hub user) · GET /api/v1/records (one row per label_revision, own is forced to scope=mine) · GET /api/v1/cases/search (own = the caller's assignments only) · GET /api/v1/cases/{series_uid}/history (own must hold an assignment on the series, else 403 not your work). One implementation with the /ui/api pages (routes_work.review_queue_data, routes_projects.start_review_as / review_as, label_states for the per-revision state); a labeler's own rows carry full patient IDs (they are their cases, as my/assignments), the data scope is masked as on the web (sees_patient_ids); refusals carry the message as both error and detail. Additive: no migration, no change to any existing route; tests/test_workspace_api.py, test_roles.py (scope rule per route)as above§12; Labeling, Review
v1.7 additions — project workspace API (§13, D98–D101; app/api_projects.py)OncoStudio's project-centric Labeling tab (D98): the projects list, a project's cases with their stage / split / facets and the project's distribution, adding cases from OncoStudio's own DICOM search, taking a case, marking the split. Same call shape and scope as §12 (user_id = the OncoStudio login, the D76 scope of the bound account; unbound → user_bound:false, empty lists, 403 on writes). D99: any bound login lists, creates a project, adds cases and self-assigns; leads (can_review) mark the split and close / reopen. Routes: GET /api/v1/roi/registry (the §3 registry — {sha256, items:[{official, aliases[], category}]} — for the ROI picker) · GET /api/v1/projects?status= (list rows with counts incl. valid/excluded, finished = D100 as amended by D143: not active (closed by a lead), my_open, labelers:[{name, login, n}], origin, created_by) · POST /api/v1/projects (the web's validation; roi_set canonicalised against the registry, unknown names kept and echoed in unregistered[]; origin='oncostudio', lead = the creator's display name, created_by = the hub username; 409 duplicate code, 422 on a key that looks like a name or birth date) · POST …/{code}/status (lead; active | inactive | archived) · GET …/{code}/cases (one row per assignment of the project, unassigned rows included: status, is_valid, round, assignee:{name, login}, drawing:{name, login, since, workstation} = an active patient_claim (the claim board's rule), labeled = the latest human revision, review = the latest verdict, roi_done/roi_total, facets = sex / vendor / population / institution bucketed as the hub's boards show them + contrast normalised contrast | noncon | unknown + age in years + slice_thickness in mm, source; plus distribution over those facets + split + status. An own caller with an assignment in the project sees full patient ids, otherwise the sees_patient_ids rule) · POST …/{code}/cases (≤ 500 items: imaging_case upserted with source='oncostudio', the given facets merged into case_meta at rank dicom — contrast normalised, the agent string kept as contrast_agent, sex as M / F; an assignment unassigned or, with assign_to_me, assigned to the caller, source='oncostudio', created_by = the login; a series already in the project counts as existing) · POST …/cases/{series_uid}/assign (D101: unassigned → mine; already mine → unchanged; another labeler's never-opened assigned → mine; opened, delivered or reviewed → 409 held by <name>; release:true gives my untouched assigned case back) · POST …/{code}/split (lead; assignment.is_valid and labeling_project.valid_set.series kept in step — the hand-marked ones listed again in manual_series, manual:true, frozen_at on the first add; a series with a stored pre-label is refused for valid:true (§11.4), a sampler-frozen series leaves only on an explicit valid:false; how the sampler treats them). v1.8 addition — account registration from OncoStudio (§14, D104; app/api_workspace.py)OncoStudio's labeling build calls POST /api/v1/users/register {user_id, password?, display_name?, workstation?} when an administrator creates an account and at every login, so a login is bound without anyone visiting the web. Idempotent, never destructive: (1) a login already bound (user_alias.oncostudio_login → active web_user) → created:false, nothing touched; (2) no web_user of that name → created (role='viewer', is_labeler=true, display_name = the given one or the login, the given password when ≥ 10 characters else a generated one — vault copy as D94 so an admin can reveal it), created:true; an inactive one → 403 hub account is disabled; (3) the login is bound with the web's bind_login rules (an unbound self / excel alias of the login is reused, else one is created; a login alias linked to another hub user → 409). One transaction; the reply is {ok, created, bound, hub_username, role, is_labeler, password_generated}; 400 on a login outside ^[A-Za-z0-9._@-]{1,64}$. Workstation bearer as §4; the OncoStudio password travels only to create the hub user. tests/test_register.pynone (web_user, user_alias as they are)§14; Labeling

POST /api/v1/projects/{code}/send-central {user_id, image_series_uids[] (≤ 100)} / POST /ui/api/projects/:id/send-central (lead) → {ok, sent[{uid, rtss_sop_uid, at}], refused[{uid, reason}], destination}. Sendable: stored status approved and not sent, the approved revision (the last approving verdict's RTSTRUCT, else the row's latest) is a human revision whose file the hub holds, and that SOP was never sent. Each send reads the image series' first file on the central share (the sync_headers path), restores the patient module (PatientName, birth date / time, sex, other ids / names, …) into a copy of the stored de-identified label, C-STOREs it with calling AE ONCOLAB_HUB through a temporary file that is deleted at once, and records central_send {assignment_id, rtss_sop_uid, sent_at, sent_by, status sent|failed, detail, destination}; on success assignment.central_sent_at (stage sent). Refusal reasons: not a case of this project, not approved, already sent, no label file, patient module unavailable (no readable image header with a PatientName — a de-identified file never reaches the central PACS), or the C-STORE failure (association refused / timed out, a non-zero status) — failures are recorded, never retried silently. Sent is final: review → 409 sent to central, assign / reviewer → final. POST /api/v1/assignments/{id}/reopen {user_id, comment} / POST /ui/api/assignments/:id/reopen (lead; comment required; only a sent row, else 409) → {ok, status:"changes_requested"}: the comment is recorded as a changes-requested verdict for the labeler, central_sent_at cleared, the row's central_send marked superseded (central.superseded:true); the corrected revision is reviewed and sent again (the same revision cannot be sent twice). D53 stands: no automated path sends to the central SCP. D166 (narrowed 2026-10-10 to the name): an unnamed ROI (named (undefined), empty or blank; a real name with ROINumber ≤ 0 is a name) is dropped from roi_names (deliverable events, uploads, PACS lists, the merge's historical revisions), from the merge and from the done counts; the label checks record UNNAMED_ROI (warning, one file) for such a file instead of any other finding of it. 1.53 — bulk plan edits PUT /ui/api/roi-board/plans (§71.3, S44, U137, D315; app/ui/routes_roadmap.py put_plans / _bulk_body next to put_plan / set_plan, app/api_workspace.py CONTRACT_VERSION; tests tests/test_roadmap_bulk_v153.py)ROI 로드맵 › the selection bar and timeline dragsLead (as §46.2). Body {rows: [{roi, modality}], set?: {status, target_date, owner, note, label_target, milestones{labeling, training, validating, released}}, shift_days?: int}: per row the §46.2 semantics — set applied as a plan body, then shift_days moves every date the plan has; one transaction, all-or-nothing; every changed field in roi_plan_log; roi_board SSE per row. Reply {ok, rows: [board rows], changed}; 422 <roi>/<modality>: <reason> with nothing applied; empty rows, > 200 rows, neither set nor shift_days, a non-integer shift_days → 422 (a bad set field → the §46.2 text, no row named). Every row is resolved first (registry name or alias, else a board row of that modality; the same ROI twice → <roi>/<modality>: listed twice), then applied in one transaction. The shift moves the dates the plan holds after set (a date set clears is not shifted; a plan without dates is untouched); a row counts in changed when ≥ 1 field changed, and only such a row sends its SSE; shift_days: 0 changes nothing. A plan left empty is deleted (its history stays) and leaves rows unless the board lists the ROI otherwise. No new table; the per-ROI route is unchanged. contract_version 1.53.—

The rows are grouped by the series' modality (imaging_case.modality) and each group is one prelabel_only job under app:hub: mode all → params {release: info.default_pipeline.release, modality, requested_by: <login>} for CT and MR rows (other modalities skipped modality PT: no default pipeline); mode models → each model resolved as for rounds (md5 → the deployed path of its highest version in model_folder), one model = the §27.4 form (model_path + model_md5 or model_job_id), several = model_paths (a JSON list, in the order given; a job_id model runs alone), plus requested_by and modality (the executor checks the models against it and scopes its in-flight rule by it, so a CT and an MR job of one project run together); a model of another modality is not sent to a group (no chosen model for modality MR). An executor whose info.contract_version is below 1.21 gets the §27.4 single-model job for all rows (no requested_by / modality params) and refuses mode all and several models. Refusals, one sentence in error (the executor's own words in executor): 503 executor unavailable, 409 pipeline unverified, no release on the executor (mode all, default_pipeline null), model not in the registry: <md5>, no rows of modality CT or MR, executor too old for the default pipeline, no chosen model fits the rows' modality, the model cannot be used: …; 400 for a bad mode / models list; the models are resolved before the rows are judged, so an unknown md5 is refused even when every row would be skipped. One group refused while another was accepted → its rows are in skipped with that sentence. The row keeps what was asked (assignment.autocontour_spec, migration 038) and every row's autocontour is {status, rtss_sop_uid, mode, release, models[{family, version, md5}], requested_by, model (the old string: default pipeline 1.7.0, Chest 2.0.1 + Abdomen 2.0.0 or the version — the request's, also once ready), at, job_id, error (failed only: the job's error, else its first failed stage, counted from 1: stage 11 of 25 (evaluate) failed (exit 1))}; rows asked before v1.21 and round pre-labels read as mode models with the stored revision's model. The §7.3 intake marks the row's previous pre-label revision superseded_by the new one (newest wins). GET /api/v1/models: items gain version (the highest of the md5's versions), latest, released {version, release}|null (the md5 is in the newest release = the highest semver release, site builds excluded), hidden (/mnt/deploy/config/hidden_models.json: a whole family, or one version), path (the deployed folder of the highest version); items' modality = the training job's, else the model fingerprint's (modality, or its git_tag model/<ct|mr>/…), else the job's project modality, null when nothing says; the reply gains families[{family, modality (the executor's release lists, else the items', else MR_* = MR / released = CT, else null), latest{md5, version}, released{md5, version, release}|null, n_versions, hidden}] (latest = the highest non-hidden version, an md5 ranked by its highest version; hidden families are listed, marked) and default_pipeline (the executor's info.default_pipeline, cached 60 s, null when it is down or older). GET /ui/api/registry/models: each model gains latest / released / hidden / path, each family hidden, modality and released by the same rule, the reply default_pipeline. v1.19 — project delete / undelete, label checks (§13, §26, D157, D159; app/project_delete.py, app/label_checks.py, jobs/label_checks.py, migrations 033 / 034)OncoStudio Labeling tab, web 프로젝트 / 검수 / 내 작업Delete: see the projects row (admin action password, soft delete, undelete restores the previous status). Label checks: jobs.label_checks (numpy, in a one-off container — never the 768 MB service) reads, per project row, the newest RTSTRUCT the hub holds for its image (rtss_file; a labeler's own save first) and derives per ROI: the file's slice step (median Δz), slices, gaps, polygon areas, fragments, volume, bounding box / extents, centroid relative to the Body/External ROI (else the centre of all contours); geometry is cached per file (label_check_metrics), so a run parses only new files. Findings (label_check, keyed by project, SOP, ROI, code): MISSING_SLICE error (a gap > 1.5 × step of 1–3 slices — a longer gap is two parts of the ROI, e.g. the discs between vertebrae; the missing z named), EMPTY_ROI, TINY_FRAGMENT (a contour < 2 % of the ROI's largest slice that no contour of the ROI on the neighbouring slices overlaps — a real island continues, a speck does not; extents leave such specks out), DUPLICATE_ROI, LATERALITY error (_L/_R centroids swapped; DICOM +x = patient left) — from one file; SINGLE_SLICE, SHAPE_OUTLIER (volume, width, depth, height — not slice count, which depends on the slice thickness; volume and height not for an ROI cut by the scan's z-edge), POSITION_OUTLIER (x / y of the centroid relative to the Body/External centroid, else the centre of all contours — not z, the Body is cut by the scan range too) — once the project has checks_min_samples (default 20, lead-editable 5–500, applies from the next run) samples of that ROI, against median ± 3 MAD (MAD scaled to σ, floored at 10 % / 10 mm). These narrow the contract's wording after the first MR_ABD run (D159 note in STATUS): the literal rules flagged natural organ islands, multi-part ROIs and the scan range. One cause, one finding: LATERALITY suppresses the pair's POSITION_OUTLIER, SINGLE_SLICE suppresses SHAPE_OUTLIER. Per-ROI statistics in label_check_roi, the row summary in label_check_row. Runs: the nightly chain (step 4 of deploy/rtss_nightly.sh), and a labeler's save queues its RTSTRUCT (label_check_queue) — every 10 min deploy/label_checks_queue.sh C-GETs it and re-checks its projects (the save never waits); --project, --rebuild, --dry-run by hand. Routes: GET /api/v1/projects/{code}/checks {min_samples, computed_at, rois[{roi, n_samples, stats_enabled, n_error, n_warning}], items[{assignment_id, image_series_uid, patient_id, stage, n_error, n_warning, top≤3}]} (findings first; ids masked as on the cases route), GET /api/v1/cases/{series}/checks {computed_at, source{kind, sop_uid}, findings[]} (project= picks the project), POST /api/v1/projects/{code}/checks/settings {user_id, min_samples} (lead); web twins /ui/api/projects/:id/checks, /ui/api/assignments/:id/checks, /ui/api/projects/:id/checks/settings. checks:{n_error, n_warning, computed_at}|null on §13 cases rows (and the web board), both review queues, 내 작업 rows and the drawer's assignment. Background jobs

User crontab on the platform host, one block written by deploy/setup.sh cron; most lines run for production and dev. Every job records its last result in ops_status (관리 › 시스템).

JobWhenWhat it doesIf it fails
reconcile_pacsevery 5 minconfirms delivered RTSTRUCTs against the PACS indexdisabled until a read-only account exists
roi_syncevery 30 minreloads the ROI registry filethe previous registry stays
sync_cfind / sync_headers:07/:37 · :17/:47; deploy/rtss_nightly.sh daily 00:50 KST under the cfind lock (D156a): sync_cfind --rtss → rtss_fetch --retry-errors (one-off 4 GB container) → setup.sh rtss-merge (8 GB); fetch + merge only before 07:00 KST with MemAvailable ≥ 12 GB, a skipped step is a STATUS line; log ~/oncolab-logs/rtss_nightly.log; --dry-run counts only. rtss_merge without --rebuild also rebuilds a row whose merged label is older than a newly fetched fileseries metadata from C-FIND and DICOM headers (read-only). D156: a series keeps the PatientID it has from the PACS — a later C-FIND that returns it under another PatientID (the PACS can file one series under two in the same study) records that one in alt_patient_ids instead of re-stamping the case; pacs_rtss carries each RTSTRUCT series' own PatientID. --rtss re-asks, per study of a resolved row in an active project (not checked for 20 h; table pacs_rtss_check), only its RTSTRUCT series (series level, no PatientID key); --before-026 re-asks once the patients last asked before the RTSTRUCT mirror existed that had studies on the PACS (one it did not know cannot hold a missed RTSTRUCT, and costs a slow PatientName query). Cron timeouts run inside the container (docker exec … timeout N python …): a timeout around docker exec killed only the client and left the job running unlocked. The RTSTRUCT list of a row (GET …/cases/{series}/rtss) matches pacs_rtss by the row's study, or by a file referencing the row's image under any PatientID the hub holds for it (case, assignment, case aliases, patient keys) — never the case patient alone; headers: at most HEADERS_MAX_PER_RUN (200) per run, pool rows of planning rounds firstcases keep their old metadata
project → dataset record (D115/D115b)one-time, 2026-10-03labeling_project.lab_dataset_id names the /mnt/deploy/data version a data-list project was trained into; the reviewed table with the evidence per project is import/project_datasets.csv; there is no job — a new dataset or project is recorded by editing that file and applying it once
fetch_headers (D119)one-time, by hand (python -m jobs.fetch_headers [--limit] [--workers 3] [--projects A,B] [--dry-run])for every patient + study of the data-list projects whose case lacks name / sex / vendor: one instance of one series by C-GET (aborted after the first image), header kept under /srv/imports/samples/, series fields + study-level values written to every case of the study, memoised in dicom_header_cache; the share-based sync_headers stays as it was
sync_deploy:05/:35 dev, :20/:50 prodregisters /mnt/deploy models and data splits; heavy hashing only 20:00–07:00; v1.5 kind cases: deployed split items + their metrics-JSON facets (allow-list: gender, age, slice thickness, vendor, population, contrast) → cases/meta, only to a hub on contract ≥ 1.5catches up on the next run
prelabel_pushevery 10 minretries pre-label C-STOREs to a project's destination (never the central PACS); rows for pull delivery (pull/pulled) are not touchedthe push stays queued, shown on the project page
round_tick:06 and every 10 minmoves labeling rounds on (sampled → labeling, pre-label job ended → labeling, auto-train at the policy's ratio + labeled validation set, finished job → evaluated with metrics); the training loop's tick (v1.28 §41: an iteration's export → pre-label jobs, evaluation → auto-deploy, items added since → one pre-label job with the current model); refreshes ai-pipeline's profile categories dailythe round waits; the round page says why
suggest02:40recomputes 추천yesterday's suggestions stay
sync_spec_profiles:13pulls ai-pipeline's regulatory profiles for 인구통계 대조the page shows the last pull
policy_tickevery 10 minfires armed training policies at the executorthe executor's refusal is shown on the policy
exec poll (in the app)every 30 sre-reads unfinished training jobs from the executor (events are the main path), then moves the training loop's iterations on (export → pre-label, evaluation → deploy; v1.28 §41)the job page says the executor is unreachable
backup03:30 UTCpg_dump to ~/oncolab-backups/ (keeps 14)an alert on 관리 › 시스템

Run, test, deploy

  1. Dev instance (:8792, database oncolab_dev, paired with the executor's dev instance): bash deploy/setup.sh dev-deploy in the hub workspace builds the image, migrates the dev and test databases and restarts only the dev container. Log in as smoke_admin.
  2. Tests: docker build -q -t oncolab-ingest:wip . && bash deploy/run_tests.sh oncolab-ingest:wip (about 2 minutes; it refuses to start with less than 12 GB free memory, because the host is shared). The count is in the hub's latest MILESTONE line; four MariaDB tests skip unless the test fixture runs (deploy/setup.sh fixture). The page gate (docs-gate.sh) runs with them.
  3. Production (:8790, database oncolab): bash deploy/setup.sh prod-only — refuses outside 20:00–07:00 KST unless no workstation has sent anything for 30 minutes, backs up the database to ~/oncolab-backups/oncolab_pre-deploy_<commit>_<time>.dump, builds, migrates and restarts only production. Check first that the executor has no running or queued stage.
  4. Rollback: retag the previous image (docker tag oncolab-ingest:<commit> oncolab-ingest) and run bash deploy/setup.sh prod-restart; migrations only add, so older code runs on the newer schema. Restore the dump only if data itself was damaged.
  5. Health: /api/v1/health on :8790 and :8792 answers status, database and version. Logs: docker logs oncolab-ingest (or -dev); cron jobs in ~/oncolab-logs/; each production container's log up to its replacement in ~/oncolab-backups/logs/ (14 days, S10).

What can go wrong

Tests refuse to startLess than 12 GB free: the host is shared with other users' work. Wait; never stop someone else's process.
"Take over did not work"Look at the item's history: an assign_refused row names who asked, their hub role and the hub's sentence (S10). assigned to X with role labeler/viewer means only a lead may take over — the OncoStudio role (Admin, UserAdmin) is not what the hub checks. No row and no request in ~/oncolab-backups/logs/ means the request never reached the hub.
A production deploy restarted the dev instancesetup.sh ingest restarts both; use prod-only for production.
Training pages say "not configured" or "unreachable"EXEC_URL is unset in that instance's env file, or the executor is down; running jobs continue and the hub catches up from events and polls.
ai-pipeline figures missing on 인구통계 대조ai-pipeline only accepts the production token pair; the dev hub reads with that pair (SPEC_PROFILES_SECRET).
A job page shows no model although one was registeredA checkpoint re-export has the same weights as an older run; the page matches by the job's own model hash.
A pre-label push times outThe destination PC is busy or off; the push is retried every 10 minutes.
Old backups disappearedThe nightly job keeps the newest 14 dumps including pre-deploy dumps; copy one elsewhere to keep it longer.
Source: onco-mlops/docs/components/hub.html · owner: labeling-server, labeling-web · edit the master and run scripts/docs-sync.sh (docs/README.md).
